```python
@needs_node
def test_a_gateway_error_does_not_claim_the_import_failed(client, tmp_path):
    # Behind a reverse proxy a long import can end in an HTML 504 while
    # missingarr still sends the command: the page must not say "failed".
    out = run_page(tmp_path, page_script(client), setup_one(proposal()) + """
route('POST', '/api/imports/1/import', () => broken(504));
await page.importDownload(inst, d);
out.gateway = { toasts: toasts.slice(), calls: calls.map(c => c[1]), counted, busy: page.busy };
toasts.length = 0; calls.length = 0;
route('POST', '/api/imports/1/import', () => { throw new TypeError('network down'); });
await page.importDownload(inst, d);
out.network = { toasts: toasts.slice(), calls: calls.map(c => c[1]) };
""")
    assert out["gateway"] == {"toasts": [[UNCLEAR, "info"]],
                              "calls": ["/api/imports/1/import", "/api/imports",
                                        "/api/imports/1/proposal?download_id=a%2F1"],
                              "counted": 1, "busy": {}}
    assert out["network"] == {"toasts": [[UNCLEAR, "info"]],
                              "calls": ["/api/imports/1/import", "/api/imports",
                                        "/api/imports/1/proposal?download_id=a%2F1"]}


@needs_node
def test_discard_with_and_without_blocklist(client, tmp_path):
    out = run_page(tmp_path, page_script(client), setup_one(proposal()) + f"""
route('POST', '/api/imports/1/discard', (url, body) => answer(200, {{ download_id: body.download_id, title: '{TITLE}', blocklist: body.blocklist, queue_id: 11 }}));
await page.discard(inst, d);
out.first = {{ confirm: confirms[0], calls: calls.map(c => [c[0], c[1], c[2]]), toasts: toasts.slice() }};
page.blocklist[page.key(inst, d)] = false;
calls.length = 0; toasts.length = 0;
await page.discard(inst, d);
out.second = {{ confirm: confirms[1], call: calls[0], toasts: toasts.slice() }};
out.counted = counted;
confirmAnswer = false; calls.length = 0;
await page.discard(inst, d);
out.cancelled = calls.length;
confirmAnswer = true; toasts.length = 0;
route('POST', '/api/imports/1/discard', () => answer(409, {{ detail: 'Already handled — this download no longer waits in the queue' }}));
await page.discard(inst, d);
route('POST', '/api/imports/1/discard', () => broken(500));
await page.discard(inst, d);
out.errors = toasts.slice();
""")
    assert out["first"]["confirm"] == f'Discard "{TITLE}"?\n\n{DISCARD_ON}'
    assert out["first"]["calls"] == [
        ["POST", "/api/imports/1/discard", {"download_id": "a/1", "blocklist": True}],
        ["GET", "/api/imports", None],
        ["GET", "/api/imports/1/proposal?download_id=a%2F1", None]]
    assert out["first"]["toasts"] == [[f"Discarded: {TITLE}", "success"]]
    assert out["second"]["confirm"] == (
        f'Discard "{TITLE}"?\n\nThe download and its files are removed from the download client. '
        "It is not blocklisted, and there is no new search.")
    assert out["second"]["call"] == ["POST", "/api/imports/1/discard", {"download_id": "a/1", "blocklist": False}]
    assert out["second"]["toasts"] == [[f"Discarded: {TITLE}", "success"]]
    assert out["counted"] == 2
    assert out["cancelled"] == 0
    assert out["errors"] == [["Already handled — this download no longer waits in the queue", "error"],
                             ["Discard failed", "error"]]


# ── The counter functions of app.js in node ──────────────────────────────────

APP_PRELUDE = r"""
const fs = require('fs');
const vm = require('vm');
globalThis.setTimeout = () => 0;
globalThis.clearTimeout = () => {};
globalThis.setInterval = () => 0;
globalThis.clearInterval = () => {};
globalThis.EventSource = function () {};
globalThis.window = globalThis;
globalThis.location = { pathname: '/imports', search: '', href: 'http://t/imports' };
const listeners = {};
const elements = {};
const element = () => ({ textContent: 'old', hidden: true });
globalThis.document = {
  addEventListener: (name, fn) => { listeners[name] = fn; },
  getElementById: () => null,
  querySelectorAll: (selector) => elements[selector] || [],
};
const stores = {};
globalThis.Alpine = { store: (name, value) => { if (value !== undefined) stores[name] = value; return stores[name]; },
                      $data: () => ({}) };
const responses = [];
const fetched = [];
globalThis.fetch = async (url, options) => { fetched.push(url); return responses.shift(); };
const source = fs.readFileSync('static/js/app.js', 'utf8');
vm.runInThisContext(source);
vm.runInThisContext(source);   // hx-boost runs the file again after every navigation
const out = {};
"""


def run_app_js(tmp_path, body):
    case = tmp_path / "case.js"
    case.write_text(APP_PRELUDE + "(async () => {\n" + body +
                    "\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(out));\n})().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });\n")
    result = subprocess.run([NODE, str(case)], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    return json.loads(result.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])


@needs_node
def test_count_texts(tmp_path):
    out = run_app_js(tmp_path, """
out.badge = [importsCountText({ total: 0, complete: true }), importsCountText({ total: 0, complete: false }),
             importsCountText({ total: 3, complete: true }), importsCountText({ total: 2, complete: false })];
out.dashboard = [importsDashboardText({ total: 0, complete: true }), importsDashboardText({ total: 0, complete: false }),
                 importsDashboardText({ total: 1, complete: true }), importsDashboardText({ total: 3, complete: true }),
                 importsDashboardText({ total: 2, complete: false })];
""")
    # "?" only stands for 0: with a number the badge shows it, the dashboard line names the missing app.
    assert out["badge"] == ["", "?", "3", "2"]
    assert out["dashboard"] == ["No imports waiting", UNKNOWN_SUMMARY, "1 import waiting", "3 imports waiting",
                                "2 imports waiting — an app could not be read or has just restarted"]


@needs_node
def test_update_fills_the_menu_badge_and_the_dashboard_line(tmp_path):
    out = run_app_js(tmp_path, """
const badges = [element(), element()]; const lines = [element()];
elements['[data-imports-count]'] = badges; elements['[data-imports-dashboard]'] = lines;
const state = () => [badges.map(b => [b.textContent, b.hidden]), lines.map(l => [l.textContent, l.hidden])];
updateImportsCount(JSON.stringify({ total: 0, complete: true, per_instance: [], checked_at: '2026-10-02T17:00:00Z' }));
out.zero = state();
updateImportsCount(JSON.stringify({ total: 0, complete: false }));
out.unknown = state();
updateImportsCount(JSON.stringify({ total: 3, complete: true }));
out.three = state();
updateImportsCount('<html>not json</html>');
out.afterJunk = state();
""")
    assert out["zero"] == [[["", True], ["", True]], [["No imports waiting", False]]]
    assert out["unknown"] == [[["?", False], ["?", False]], [[UNKNOWN_SUMMARY, False]]]
    assert out["three"] == [[["3", False], ["3", False]], [["3 imports waiting", False]]]
    assert out["afterJunk"] == out["three"]


@needs_node
def test_refresh_reads_the_count_and_swallows_errors(tmp_path):
    out = run_app_js(tmp_path, """
const badge = element(); elements['[data-imports-count]'] = [badge];
responses.push({ status: 200, ok: true, redirected: false, url: '/api/imports/count',
                 text: async () => JSON.stringify({ total: 2, complete: true }) });
await refreshImportsCount();
out.first = [fetched.slice(), badge.textContent, badge.hidden];
responses.push({ status: 502, ok: false, redirected: false, url: '/api/imports/count', text: async () => 'bad' });
await refreshImportsCount();
out.afterError = [badge.textContent, badge.hidden];
globalThis.fetch = async () => { throw new TypeError('network down'); };
await refreshImportsCount();
out.afterNetwork = badge.textContent;
globalThis.fetch = async (url) => ({ status: 401, ok: false, redirected: false, url });
await refreshImportsCount();
out.login = location.href;
""")
    assert out["first"] == [["/api/imports/count"], "2", False]
    assert out["afterError"] == ["2", False]
    assert out["afterNetwork"] == "2"
    assert out["login"] == "/login?next=%2Fimports"
```

Dazu in `tests/test_p4_pages.py` die Pfadliste von `test_no_page_contains_the_api_key` (Zeilen 49-50) um `/imports` ergänzen:

```python
    for path in ("/", "/instances", f"/instances/{instance_id}/edit", f"/instances/{instance_id}/card",
                 "/instances/new", "/history", "/logs", "/searched", "/imports"):
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**

```bash
.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_h5_imports_page.py tests/test_p4_pages.py -q -p no:cacheprovider
```

Erwartet: `22 failed, 6 passed`. Die 22 Tests in h5 scheitern so:
- Seitentests: `AssertionError`, weil der Menüeintrag `href="/imports" class="nav-link active"`, das Element `data-imports-count`, die Dashboard-Zeile, die Hilfe-Karte und die CSS-Regel fehlen und das Gerüst keine Komponente hat; `test_menu_has_the_imports_link_and_counter_on_every_page` scheitert schon vorher mit `ValueError: '/imports' is not in list`.
- `test_tooltips_explain_the_verdict_and_the_blocklist`: `KeyError: 'imports_verdict'`.
- Node-Tests der Seite: `ValueError: substring not found` in `component_script` (es gibt noch kein `function importsPage()`).
- Node-Tests von `app.js`: `AssertionError` mit `ReferenceError: importsCountText is not defined` (bzw. `updateImportsCount`, `refreshImportsCount`) in stderr.

Die 6 Tests in `tests/test_p4_pages.py` laufen schon durch: Task 6 hat die Route gebaut, und das Gerüst enthält keinen Schlüssel. Die neue Zeile schützt nur vor Rückschritten.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

**3a.** `templates/imports.html` ganz ersetzen. Muster ist `templates/checked_search.html` (Inline-Skript mit `var` und `function`-Deklarationen vor der Komponente, weil hx-boost das Skript bei jedem Seitenwechsel neu ausführt). `x-init="load()"` ist richtig, weil die Methode `load` heißt, nicht `init` (vgl. `tests/test_p5_lists.py:101-103`).

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% block title %}Imports — {{ app_name }}{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<script>
var IMPORTS_VERDICTS = {
    fits: ['badge-online', 'fits'],
    foreign: ['badge-error', 'foreign?'],
    unknown: ['badge-unknown', 'cannot judge']
};
var IMPORTS_POLL_MS = 3000;
var IMPORTS_POLL_BUDGET_MS = 120000;
var IMPORTS_PARALLEL_PROPOSALS = 2;

function importsPage() {
    return {
        loading: true,
        instances: [],
        checkedAt: '',
        // key -> {loading, error, data}; data is the proposal JSON of /api/imports/<id>/proposal
        proposals: {},
        // key -> false when "Blocklist and search again" is unticked (missing = ticked)
        blocklist: {},
        busy: {},
        _request: 0,

        key(inst, d) { return inst.id + ':' + d.download_id; },

        async load() {
            const request = ++this._request;
            this.loading = true;
            try {
                const resp = await apiFetch('/api/imports');
                if (request !== this._request) return;
                if (!resp.ok) {
                    toast(await errorDetail(resp, 'Could not load the imports'), 'error');
                    return;
                }
                const data = await resp.json();
                this.instances = Array.isArray(data.instances) ? data.instances : [];
                this.checkedAt = data.checked_at || '';
                this.proposals = {};
            } catch (err) {
                if (!isSessionExpired(err)) toast('Could not load the imports', 'error');
                return;
            } finally {
                if (request === this._request) this.loading = false;
            }
            await this.loadProposals();
        },

        // The app runs ffprobe on every video file of a proposal: read them one
        // by one, at most IMPORTS_PARALLEL_PROPOSALS at a time, in list order.
        async loadProposals() {
            const request = this._request;
            const queue = [];
            for (const inst of this.instances) {
                for (const d of (inst.downloads || [])) queue.push([inst, d]);
            }
            const worker = async () => {
                while (queue.length && request === this._request) {
                    const [inst, d] = queue.shift();
                    await this.loadProposal(inst, d);
                }
            };
            const workers = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(IMPORTS_PARALLEL_PROPOSALS, queue.length); i++) workers.push(worker());
            await Promise.all(workers);
        },

        async loadProposal(inst, d) {
            const k = this.key(inst, d);
            this.proposals[k] = { loading: true, error: '', data: null };
            try {
                const resp = await apiFetch(
                    `/api/imports/${inst.id}/proposal?download_id=${encodeURIComponent(d.download_id)}`);
                if (resp.status === 409) {
                    // Handled meanwhile (by the app, an auto-import or another tab).
                    inst.downloads = inst.downloads.filter(x => x.download_id !== d.download_id);
                    delete this.proposals[k];
                    return;
                }
                if (!resp.ok) {
                    this.proposals[k] = { loading: false, error: await errorDetail(resp, 'Could not read the proposal'), data: null };
                    return;
                }
                this.proposals[k] = { loading: false, error: '', data: await resp.json() };
            } catch (err) {
                if (isSessionExpired(err)) return;
                this.proposals[k] = { loading: false, error: 'Could not read the proposal', data: null };
            }
        },

        proposal(inst, d) {
            const entry = this.proposals[this.key(inst, d)];
            return entry && entry.data ? entry.data : null;
        },
        canImport(inst, d) {
            const p = this.proposal(inst, d);
            return !!p && p.importable === true && !this.busy[this.key(inst, d)];
        },
        importTitle(inst, d) {
            const entry = this.proposals[this.key(inst, d)] || {};
            if (this.busy[this.key(inst, d)]) return 'An action is running';
            if (!entry.data) return entry.error ? 'No proposal: ' + entry.error : "Waiting for the app's proposal";
            return entry.data.importable ? "Import the app's proposal" : entry.data.why_not;
        },
        verdictOf(inst, d) {
            const p = this.proposal(inst, d);
            return p && p.verdict ? p.verdict : {};
        },
        discardClass(inst, d) {
            return this.verdictOf(inst, d).state === 'foreign' ? 'btn-danger' : 'btn-secondary';
        },
        // Sonarr keeps a download whose episodes were not all imported.
        coverageNote(inst, d) {
            const p = this.proposal(inst, d);
            const missing = p ? Number(p.uncovered_episodes) || 0 : 0;
            return missing > 0
                ? `The proposal covers fewer episodes than the download (${missing} missing); the app keeps the download afterwards.`
                : '';
        },

        async importDownload(inst, d) {
            const k = this.key(inst, d);
            const p = this.proposal(inst, d);
            if (!p || !p.importable || this.busy[k]) return;
            const v = this.verdictOf(inst, d);
            let text = `Import "${d.title}" as ${p.target}?`;
            if (v.state === 'foreign') {
                text += `\n\nThe pre-filter rules object: ${(v.reasons || []).join(', ')}`;
                if ((v.details || []).length) text += ` (${v.details.join('; ')})`;
                text += '.';
            }
            const note = this.coverageNote(inst, d);
            if (note) text += '\n\n' + note;
            text += '\n\nThe app moves the files into the library.';
            if (!confirm(text)) return;
            // An answer without missingarr's JSON detail (a reverse proxy's 504,
            // a lost connection) says nothing about the command: it may still run.
            const unclear = 'No clear answer — the import may still run in the app; check the queue';
            this.busy[k] = true;
            try {
                const resp = await apiFetch(`/api/imports/${inst.id}/import`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ download_id: d.download_id, proposal_key: p.proposal_key })
                });
                if (!resp.ok) {
                    const detail = await errorDetail(resp, '');
                    if (detail) toast(detail, 'error');
                    else toast(unclear, 'info');
                } else {
                    const started = await resp.json();
                    const result = await this.pollCommand(inst, started.command_id);
                    if (result.state === 'imported') toast(`Imported: ${d.title}`, 'success');
                    else if (result.state === 'failed') toast(`Import failed: ${result.message}`, 'error');
                    else if (result.state === 'running') toast('Still running in the app — check the queue later', 'info');
                    else toast(result.message, 'info');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                if (isSessionExpired(err)) return;
                toast(unclear, 'info');
            } finally {
                delete this.busy[k];
            }
            await this.load();
            await refreshImportsCount();
        },

        // Asks every IMPORTS_POLL_MS until the command is no longer running or
        // IMPORTS_POLL_BUDGET_MS is used up. Disk commands run one after another
        // in the app, so "still running" is no error.
        async pollCommand(inst, commandId) {
            const deadline = Date.now() + IMPORTS_POLL_BUDGET_MS;
            let last = { command_id: commandId, state: 'running', status: '', message: '' };
            while (Date.now() < deadline) {
                await this.sleep(IMPORTS_POLL_MS);
                try {
                    const resp = await apiFetch(`/api/imports/${inst.id}/commands/${commandId}`);
                    if (resp.ok) {
                        last = await resp.json();
                        if (last.state !== 'running') return last;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    if (isSessionExpired(err)) throw err;
                }
            }
            return Object.assign({}, last, { state: 'running' });
        },

        async discard(inst, d) {
            const k = this.key(inst, d);
            if (this.busy[k]) return;
            const blocklist = this.blocklist[k] !== false;
            const text = blocklist
                ? `Discard "${d.title}"?\n\nThe download and its files are removed from the download client. If the app grabbed this release itself, it marks it as failed, puts it on the blocklist and may search again (Radarr only for a monitored, available movie; Sonarr as its Redownload Failed settings allow). A download added by hand is only removed.`
                : `Discard "${d.title}"?\n\nThe download and its files are removed from the download client. It is not blocklisted, and there is no new search.`;
            if (!confirm(text)) return;
            this.busy[k] = true;
            try {
                const resp = await apiFetch(`/api/imports/${inst.id}/discard`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ download_id: d.download_id, blocklist: blocklist })
                });
                if (resp.ok) toast(`Discarded: ${d.title}`, 'success');
                else toast(await errorDetail(resp, 'Discard failed'), 'error');
            } catch (err) {
                if (isSessionExpired(err)) return;
                toast('Discard failed', 'error');
            } finally {
                delete this.busy[k];
            }
            await this.load();
            await refreshImportsCount();
        },

        // The app's own queue on the host this page came from; port and base
        // path come from the instance URL (the URL itself may be a Docker name).
        queueHref(inst) {
            const l = inst.queue_link || {};
            return `${l.scheme || 'http'}://${location.hostname}${l.port ? ':' + l.port : ''}${l.path || '/activity/queue'}`;
        },
        sleep(ms) { return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms)); },
        verdictClass(v) { return 'badge ' + (IMPORTS_VERDICTS[v && v.state] || IMPORTS_VERDICTS.unknown)[0]; },
        verdictLabel(v) { return (IMPORTS_VERDICTS[v && v.state] || IMPORTS_VERDICTS.unknown)[1]; },
        fmtSize(bytes) { return bytes ? (bytes / 1073741824).toFixed(2) + ' GiB' : '—'; },
        fmtAge(iso) {
            const t = Date.parse(iso || '');
            if (Number.isNaN(t)) return 'age unknown';
            const minutes = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - t) / 60000));
            if (minutes < 60) return minutes + ' min ago';
            const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
            return hours < 48 ? hours + ' h ago' : Math.floor(hours / 24) + ' days ago';
        },
        fmtTime(s) { return s ? s.replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16) + ' UTC' : '—'; },
        appName(inst) { return inst.type === 'radarr' ? 'Radarr' : 'Sonarr'; }
    };
}
</script>

<div x-data="importsPage()" x-init="load()">

<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:0.75rem;">
    <div>
        <h1 style="margin:0;font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:700;">Imports</h1>
        <p style="margin:0.25rem 0 0;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.85rem;">
            Downloads Radarr and Sonarr hold back for a manual import.
            <span x-show="checkedAt" x-text="'Read ' + fmtTime(checkedAt) + '.'"></span>
        </p>
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" @click="load()" :disabled="loading">Reload</button>
</div>

<template x-if="loading && instances.length === 0">
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;padding:3rem 1rem;color:var(--text-muted);">Loading…</div>
</template>
<template x-if="!loading && instances.length === 0">
    <div class="card" style="text-align:center;padding:3rem 1rem;color:var(--text-muted);">No instances yet.</div>
</template>

<template x-for="inst in instances" :key="inst.id">
    <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:1rem;">
        <div class="card-header" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap;">
            <strong x-text="inst.name"></strong>
            <span class="type-badge" :class="'type-' + inst.type" x-text="inst.type"></span>
            <a class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="margin-left:auto;" hx-boost="false" target="_blank" rel="noopener"
               :href="queueHref(inst)" x-text="'Queue in ' + appName(inst)"></a>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <template x-if="!inst.enabled">
                <div style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.9rem;">Switched off — not read.</div>
            </template>
            <template x-if="inst.enabled && inst.error">
                <div style="color:var(--red);font-size:0.9rem;" x-text="inst.error"></div>
            </template>
            <template x-if="inst.enabled && !inst.error && !inst.starting && inst.downloads.length === 0">
                <div style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.9rem;">Nothing open.</div>
            </template>
            <template x-if="inst.enabled && !inst.error && inst.starting && inst.downloads.length === 0">
                <div style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.9rem;">The app started less than 2 minutes ago — its queue may not be filled yet.</div>
            </template>

            <template x-for="d in inst.downloads" :key="d.download_id">
                <div style="border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:0.75rem;margin-bottom:0.75rem;">
                    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:0.75rem;flex-wrap:wrap;">
                        <div style="min-width:0;flex:1;">
                            <div style="font-weight:600;word-break:break-all;" x-text="d.title"></div>
                            <div style="font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:0.2rem;">
                                <span x-text="fmtSize(d.size)"></span> ·
                                <span x-text="fmtAge(d.added)"></span> ·
                                <span x-text="d.download_client || 'unknown client'"></span>
                            </div>
                            <ul style="margin:0.4rem 0 0;padding-left:1.1rem;font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-secondary);">
                                <template x-for="(m, i) in d.messages" :key="i">
                                    <li x-text="m"></li>
                                </template>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-end;gap:0.4rem;">
                            <div style="display:flex;gap:0.5rem;">
                                <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" :disabled="!canImport(inst, d)"
                                        :title="importTitle(inst, d)" @click="importDownload(inst, d)">Import</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-sm" :class="discardClass(inst, d)" :disabled="!!busy[key(inst, d)]"
                                        @click="discard(inst, d)">Discard</button>
                            </div>
                            <label style="font-size:0.78rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.35rem;">
                                <input type="checkbox" :checked="blocklist[key(inst, d)] !== false"
                                       @change="blocklist[key(inst, d)] = $event.target.checked">
                                Blocklist and search again
                                <span class="tooltip-icon" data-tooltip="{{ tooltips.imports_discard_blocklist }}">?</span>
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style="margin-top:0.75rem;">
                        <template x-if="(proposals[key(inst, d)] || {}).loading">
                            <div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-muted);">Reading the app's proposal… (the app checks every video file, this can take a while)</div>
                        </template>
                        <template x-if="(proposals[key(inst, d)] || {}).error">
                            <div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--red);" x-text="proposals[key(inst, d)].error"></div>
                        </template>
                        <template x-if="proposal(inst, d)">
                            <div>
                                <div style="font-size:0.85rem;margin-bottom:0.4rem;">
                                    Proposal: <strong x-text="proposal(inst, d).target"></strong>
                                </div>
                                <div style="overflow:auto;">
                                    <table class="table" style="font-size:0.78rem;">
                                        <thead><tr><th>File</th><th>Target</th><th>Quality</th><th>Languages</th><th>Group</th><th>Objections of the app</th></tr></thead>
                                        <tbody>
                                            <template x-for="(c, i) in proposal(inst, d).candidates" :key="i">
                                                <tr :style="c.video ? '' : 'color:var(--text-muted);'">
                                                    <td style="word-break:break-all;" :title="c.path" x-text="c.relative_path || c.path"></td>
                                                    <td x-text="c.video ? c.target : 'not a video — ignored'"></td>
                                                    <td x-text="c.quality || '—'"></td>
                                                    <td x-text="(c.languages || []).join(', ') || '—'"></td>
                                                    <td x-text="c.release_group || '—'"></td>
                                                    <td x-text="(c.rejections || []).join('; ') || '—'"></td>
                                                </tr>
                                            </template>
                                        </tbody>
                                    </table>
                                </div>
                                <div x-show="!proposal(inst, d).importable" style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--yellow);margin-top:0.4rem;"
                                     x-text="'Import is locked: ' + proposal(inst, d).why_not"></div>
                                <div x-show="coverageNote(inst, d)" style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--yellow);margin-top:0.4rem;"
                                     x-text="coverageNote(inst, d)"></div>
                                <div style="margin-top:0.5rem;font-size:0.8rem;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:0.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap;">
                                    <span :class="verdictClass(verdictOf(inst, d))" x-text="verdictLabel(verdictOf(inst, d))"></span>
                                    <span class="tooltip-icon" data-tooltip="{{ tooltips.imports_verdict }}">?</span>
                                    <div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;color:var(--text-secondary);">
                                        <div x-show="(verdictOf(inst, d).reasons || []).length"
                                             x-text="'Reasons: ' + (verdictOf(inst, d).reasons || []).join(', ')"></div>
                                        <template x-for="(t, i) in (verdictOf(inst, d).details || [])" :key="i">
                                            <div x-text="t"></div>
                                        </template>
                                        <div x-show="(verdictOf(inst, d).notes || []).length"
                                             x-text="'Notes: ' + (verdictOf(inst, d).notes || []).join('; ')"></div>
                                        <div x-show="verdictOf(inst, d).error" x-text="verdictOf(inst, d).error"></div>
                                        <div x-show="inst.type === 'sonarr'" style="color:var(--text-muted);">Rules S1–S4, not measured; the title is only compared when Sonarr maps the name to a series.</div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </template>
        </div>
    </div>
</template>

</div>
{% endblock %}
```

**3d.** `templates/help.html`: neue Karte zwischen der Karte „Checked search (pre-filter)“ (endet in Zeile 33) und `<!-- Fields reference -->` (Zeile 35) einfügen.

```html
<!-- Imports -->
<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:1rem;">
    <div class="card-header"><strong>Imports</strong></div>
    <div class="card-body" style="font-size:0.9rem;line-height:1.7;color:var(--text-secondary);">
        <p>Radarr and Sonarr hold back some finished downloads instead of importing them ("Manual Import Required" in their queue): the release was matched only by ID at the grab, its name fits no series, season or episode are invalid, or a file was rejected. The <a href="/imports">Imports</a> page lists these downloads of every enabled instance, each with the app's proposal (target, quality, languages, release group and the app's objections) and missingarr's own verdict with the pre-filter rules: "fits", "foreign?" or "cannot judge". The number next to "Imports" in the menu and on the dashboard counts them; it shows "?" instead of 0 while an app cannot be read or started less than two minutes ago (its queue is still empty then, and its box on the page says so).</p>
        <p><strong>Import</strong> takes the app's proposal as it is. It is locked when there is no video file, a video file has no target or no quality, two files point to the same movie or episode, or the app objects to a file — also "Not an upgrade for existing … file": the import command ignores every objection and would replace a file that is as good or better. Import such a download in the app if you want it anyway. Right before it sends the import, missingarr reads the queue and the proposal again and builds the command itself; if the download is gone or the proposal changed, nothing is sent. The verdict never locks Import; with "foreign?" the confirmation repeats the reasons. When a Sonarr proposal covers fewer episodes than the download, the card and the confirmation say so: Sonarr keeps the download afterwards.</p>
        <p><strong>Discard</strong> removes the download and its files from the download client. With "Blocklist and search again" (the default): if the app grabbed this release itself, it marks it as failed, puts it on the blocklist and may search again (Radarr only for a monitored, available movie; Sonarr as its Redownload Failed settings allow); a download added by hand is only removed. Without it the download is only removed: it is not blocklisted, and there is no new search. Discard waits while an import of the download is queued or running in the app.</p>
        <p>"Queue in Sonarr" / "Queue in Radarr" opens the app's own queue on the host you opened missingarr on, with the port and base path of the instance URL. It only works when the app's port is reachable on that host.</p>
    </div>
</div>
```

```css
[hidden] { display: none !important; }
```

**3g.** `static/js/app.js`: nach Zeile 384 (Ende von `toggleInstance`) anhängen. Nur Funktionsdeklarationen, kein `let`/`const`/`class` auf oberster Ebene (Zeilen 1-3 der Datei erklären, warum).

```javascript

// ── Imports counter (menu badge and dashboard line) ──────────────────────────
// Filled from GET /api/imports/count: by the htmx poll in the menu and after
// every action on the Imports page. "?" stands for 0 while an app could not be
// read or has just restarted.
function importsCountText(data) {
    const total = Number(data && data.total) || 0;
    if (total > 0) return String(total);
    return data && data.complete === true ? '' : '?';
}

function importsDashboardText(data) {
    const total = Number(data && data.total) || 0;
    const complete = !!data && data.complete === true;
    if (total === 0) {
        return complete ? 'No imports waiting' : 'Imports: unknown — an app could not be read or has just restarted';
    }
    const waiting = total === 1 ? '1 import waiting' : `${total} imports waiting`;
    // The badge shows only the number; the dashboard line says when an app is missing from it.
    return complete ? waiting : `${waiting} — an app could not be read or has just restarted`;
}

function updateImportsCount(responseText) {
    let data;
    try {
        data = JSON.parse(responseText);
    } catch (_) {
        return;
    }
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return;
    const text = importsCountText(data);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-imports-count]').forEach(el => {
        el.textContent = text;
        el.hidden = text === '';
    });
    const summary = importsDashboardText(data);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-imports-dashboard]').forEach(el => {
        el.textContent = summary;
        el.hidden = false;
    });
}

async function refreshImportsCount() {
    try {
        const resp = await apiFetch('/api/imports/count');
        if (resp.ok) updateImportsCount(await resp.text());
    } catch (_) {
        // A lost session is handled by apiFetch; the next poll tries again.
    }
}
```

```python
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


def test_readme_documents_checked_search():
    readme = (ROOT / "README.md").read_text()
    assert "### Checked search" in readme
    assert "### Profile changes" in readme
    assert "**Search again after profile changes**" in readme
    assert "Interactive Search" in readme   # GET /release asks other indexers than the command
    assert "**Search again if still missing after (days)**" in readme
    assert "Redownload Failed from Interactive Search" in readme
    assert "## Upgrading to 0.9.0" in readme
    assert "/api/checked-search.csv" in readme
    assert "downloadUrl" in readme   # the security note says it is never stored


def test_readme_and_changelog_document_imports():
    readme = (ROOT / "README.md").read_text()
    for text in ("## Imports", "## Upgrading to 0.10.0", "/api/imports/count", "/activity/queue", "Not an upgrade",
                 "180 seconds", "`gomaaz/missingarr:0.10.0`"):
        assert text in readme, text
    assert readme.index("### Profile changes") < readme.index("## Imports") < readme.index("## Example: Typical Home Setup")
    assert readme.index("## Upgrading to 0.10.0") < readme.index("## Upgrading to 0.9.0")
    changelog = (ROOT / "CHANGELOG.md").read_text()
    assert "## [0.10.0]" in changelog and "180 seconds" in changelog
    assert changelog.index("## [Unreleased]") < changelog.index("## [0.10.0]") < changelog.index("## [0.9.0]")


def test_no_host_details_in_new_files():
    files = [ROOT / "README.md", *sorted((ROOT / "backend" / "checked_search").glob("*.py")),
             ROOT / "backend" / "db" / "checked_search_log.py", ROOT / "backend" / "skills" / "profiles.py",
             ROOT / "templates" / "checked_search.html", *sorted((ROOT / "tests").glob("test_g*_*.py")),
             *sorted((ROOT / "backend" / "imports").glob("*.py")), ROOT / "backend" / "api" / "imports.py",
             ROOT / "templates" / "imports.html", *sorted((ROOT / "tests").glob("test_h*_*.py")),
             ROOT / "tests" / "imports_fake_arr.py", ROOT / "CHANGELOG.md"]
    for path in files:
        text = path.read_text()
        for marker in ("/root/", "/home/", "/tmp/", "/mnt/", "/srv/"):
            assert marker not in text, f"{marker} in {path.name}"
```

```markdown
## [0.10.0] - 2026-10-02

### Added

- **Imports page** (`/imports`, menu entry "Imports" after "Pre-filter"): the downloads Radarr and Sonarr hold back for a manual import ("Manual Import Required": `importBlocked`, or `importPending` with a warning), for every enabled instance. One card per download (Sonarr's episode entries of one download are grouped) with release name, size, age, download client and the app's messages. Below it the app's proposal (`GET /api/v3/manualimport`, loaded card by card, two at a time, kept for 60 seconds) with target, quality, languages, release group and the app's objections per file, and missingarr's own verdict.
- **Import** takes the app's proposal as it is, after a confirmation. It is locked while there is no video file, a video file has no target or no quality, two files point to the same movie or episode, or the app objects to a file (also "Not an upgrade for existing … file": the import command ignores the app's objections and would replace a file that is as good or better). When a Sonarr proposal covers fewer episodes than the download, the card and the confirmation say so (Sonarr keeps the download afterwards). missingarr sends `ManualImport` with `importMode` `auto` and follows the command for up to 2 minutes: imported; partly imported (Sonarr: the download is still in the queue, some or all files were not imported); failed (with the app's message; for Radarr also when the download is still in the queue afterwards: the app ran the import but imported no file, Radarr has no partial import); unknown; or still running.
- **Discard** removes the download and its files from the download client, after a confirmation. With "Blocklist and search again" (on by default): if the app grabbed this release itself, it marks it as failed, puts it on the blocklist and may search again (Radarr only for a monitored, available movie; Sonarr as its Redownload Failed settings allow); a download added by hand is only removed. A release missingarr's checked search grabbed counts as an interactive grab: the app only searches again for it when *Redownload Failed from Interactive Search* is on. Without the blocklist the download is only removed: not blocklisted, no new search. Discard waits (`409`) while an import of the download is queued or running in the app.
- **Own verdict** with the rules of the checked search: "fits", "foreign?" (with reasons and details) or "cannot judge" (with the reason). Radarr: `/parse` and the movie rules. Sonarr: `/parse`, series, episodes and the publish date from the grab history, rules S1–S4 for every episode, and a note when the season or episode numbers in the name differ. The release of the existing file only gives a note. The verdict never locks Import.
- **Counter** of waiting downloads next to "Imports" in the menu and as a line on the dashboard, refreshed every 60 seconds. It shows "?" instead of 0 while an app cannot be read or started less than 2 minutes ago (its queue is still empty then; the app's box on the page says so instead of "Nothing open."). With a number, the dashboard line adds a note when an app is missing from it. Each app is read for it at most once a minute: `GET /api/v3/queue/status` first (and `GET /api/v3/system/status` while nothing waits), the queue only when its warning or error flags are set.
- A link per instance to the app's own queue (`/activity/queue`) on the host missingarr was opened on, with the scheme, port and base path of the instance URL.
- Help card "Imports" and info icons for the verdict and the blocklist switch.
- API: `GET /api/imports`, `GET /api/imports/count`, `GET /api/imports/<id>/proposal?download_id=…`, `POST /api/imports/<id>/import`, `GET /api/imports/<id>/commands/<command_id>`, `POST /api/imports/<id>/discard`. Every import and discard writes a line to the activity log (skill `imports`).

### Security

- The page and `/api/imports/*` sit behind the login; Import and Discard are POST requests and covered by the cross-site check.
- missingarr trusts nothing from the browser but the download id and the proposal key. Right before an import it reads the queue and the proposal again (no cache), checks every condition again and builds the `ManualImport` command itself. A download that is gone, a changed proposal, an import while a `ManualImport` with the same files runs in the app and a discard while an import of the download is queued or running are refused with `409`.
- From the app's grab history only `publishedDate` is taken. `data` (download links with your indexer's API key) is never stored, logged, shown or returned.
- API keys never appear in answers or error texts. A wrong API key of an app answers `502` "Invalid API key", not `401`, which the page would read as an expired session.

### Upgrade notes

- No database change.
- The page and the counter read the queues of the enabled apps live (the counter at most once a minute per app).
- Import moves the files into the library (`importMode` `auto`); Discard removes the download and its files from the download client. Both ask first.
- Behind a reverse proxy, allow a read timeout of at least 180 seconds for `/api/imports/`: the app runs ffprobe before it answers, and an import reads the proposal again before it sends the command. If the page gets no clear answer, it says the import may still run in the app.
- The queue link needs the apps' ports to be reachable on the same host as missingarr.
- If an external notification links to `/imports`, you can switch off the app's own notification "On Manual Interaction Required".
```

(2) Neuer Abschnitt nach dem Absatz unter `### Profile changes` (Zeile 131) und vor `## Example: Typical Home Setup` (Zeile 133). Er nennt auch, was die Seite bewusst nicht zeigt (Downloads in anderen Zuständen). Vor und nach dem Abschnitt steht je eine Leerzeile.

```markdown
## Imports

Radarr and Sonarr hold back some finished downloads instead of importing them. Their queue shows them as "Manual Import Required". Typical reasons: the release was matched to the movie or series only by ID at the grab, its name fits no series ("Unknown Series"), season or episode are invalid, or a file was rejected. The **Imports** page lists these downloads of every enabled instance, so you do not have to open each one in the app.

What the page lists:

- Every queue entry that is `importBlocked`, or `importPending` with a warning (a single rejected file keeps a download there for good otherwise). The queue is read page by page with `includeUnknownSeriesItems` / `includeUnknownMovieItems`, so "Unknown Series" entries are included. Releases a delay profile holds back never count. Downloads in other states (still downloading with a warning, failed, importing) are not shown.
- One card per download. Sonarr has one queue entry per episode; entries with the same `downloadId` form one card. A card shows the release name, size, age, download client and the app's messages.
- An instance that is switched off shows "Switched off — not read." If an app cannot be reached, only its box shows the error.

**The proposal.** For each download missingarr asks the app what it would import (`GET /api/v3/manualimport?downloadId=…&filterExistingFiles=true`). The app runs ffprobe on every video file, so one answer can take more than 30 seconds. The cards load their proposal one after another, two at a time, with a 120 second timeout, and each answer is kept for 60 seconds. A file counts as a video when it has a video extension, no "sample" in its relative path (folders included) and at least 50 MiB. Other files are shown in grey and ignored. When a Sonarr proposal covers fewer episodes than the queue entries of the download, the card and the confirmation say so: Sonarr keeps the download after the import.

**Import** takes the app's proposal as it is. The button is locked, and names the reason, unless all of these hold:

1. There is at least one video file.
2. Every video file has a target: Radarr a movie; Sonarr a series, a season and at least one episode.
3. Every video file has a quality ("Unknown" does not count).
4. No movie or episode gets two files. The import command does not check that; the second file would replace the first.
5. The app has no objection to any file. The import command ignores every objection, also "Not an upgrade for existing … file", and would replace a file that is as good or better. Import such a download in the app if you want it anyway.

A proposal without target, quality and objections is the app's answer when it could not read the file. It stays locked (condition 2).

**The verdict.** missingarr also checks the release with the rules of the checked search and shows "fits", "foreign?" (with reasons and details) or "cannot judge" (with the reason). It uses the rule settings of the instance. Radarr: `/parse` and the movie, then the movie rules. Sonarr: `/parse`, the series, the episodes and the publish date of the grab (`GET /api/v3/history?downloadId=…&eventType=1`, only `publishedDate` is taken from it), then rules S1–S4 for every episode; a different season or episode number in the release name is a note. The release of the existing file only gives a note, since the download is already there. The Sonarr verdict is weaker: its rules were not measured, and the title is only compared when Sonarr maps the release name to a series. The verdict never locks Import. With "foreign?" the confirmation repeats the reasons and Discard is highlighted.

Import, step by step:

1. You confirm. The page sends the download id and a key of the proposal it showed.
2. missingarr reads the queue and the proposal again, without cache. If the download is gone, the proposal changed or a condition fails, nothing is sent and the page says why (`409`).
3. If a `ManualImport` with one of these files is queued or running in the app already, nothing is sent either.
4. missingarr builds the `ManualImport` command itself, with the fields the app's own interface sends and `importMode` `auto` (SABnzbd downloads are moved). The page asks for the result every 3 seconds for up to 2 minutes: imported; partly imported (Sonarr: the download is still in the queue afterwards, some or all files were not imported, check it in the app); failed, with the app's message (a missing source file, for example because an auto-import was faster, stops the command; files before it may already be imported). Radarr has no partial import: a Radarr download still in the queue afterwards is "failed" (the app ran the import but imported no file, see its log). Further: unknown (the app restarted or no longer knows the command); or still running (the app runs disk commands one after another).
5. The activity log gets a line (skill `imports`) with release, target, command id and result.

**Discard** removes the download and its files from the download client (`DELETE /api/v3/queue/<id>?removeFromClient=true&blocklist=…&skipRedownload=false&changeCategory=false`; one queue id removes the whole download, all episode entries included). With "Blocklist and search again" (the default): if the app grabbed this release itself, it marks it as failed, puts it on the blocklist and may search again (Radarr only for a monitored, available movie; Sonarr as its Redownload Failed settings allow); a download added by hand is only removed. A release the checked search grabbed counts as an interactive grab: the app only searches again for it when *Redownload Failed from Interactive Search* is on (see *Checked search* for why to switch that off). Without the blocklist the download is only removed: not blocklisted, no new search. While an import of the download is queued or running in the app (`GET /api/v3/command`), Discard waits and says so (`409`): removing the files would make that import fail. A download that is gone already answers "Already handled".

**The counter.** The menu shows the number of waiting downloads next to "Imports", the dashboard a line such as "2 imports waiting". missingarr first asks `GET /api/v3/queue/status` and reads the queue only when its warning or error flags are set. Each app is read for the counter at most once a minute, however many tabs are open; an import or discard resets the count of its instance. Right after an app starts its queue is empty for a while: for 2 minutes after its start time (`GET /api/v3/system/status`) the counter shows "?" instead of 0, and the app's box on the page says "The app started less than 2 minutes ago" instead of "Nothing open.". The counter also shows "?" instead of 0 while an app cannot be read. With a number, the dashboard line adds "an app could not be read or has just restarted" when an app is missing from it.

**Queue link.** "Queue in Sonarr" / "Queue in Radarr" opens the app's own queue (`/activity/queue`) on the host you opened missingarr on, with the scheme, port and base path of the instance URL. The instance URL itself may be a Docker name your browser cannot reach. The link works when the apps' ports are reachable on the same host as missingarr; otherwise open the queue in the app yourself. External scripts and notifications can link to `/imports`.

**Behind a reverse proxy**, allow a read timeout of at least 180 seconds for `/api/imports/`: the app runs ffprobe before it answers, and an import reads the proposal again before it sends the command. If the page gets no clear answer (for example the proxy's own `504`), it does not report a failed import but says the import may still run in the app; check the queue there before you discard.

The API: `GET /api/imports` (every instance with its downloads), `GET /api/imports/count`, `GET /api/imports/<id>/proposal?download_id=…`, `POST /api/imports/<id>/import` (`{"download_id": …, "proposal_key": …}`), `GET /api/imports/<id>/commands/<command_id>`, `POST /api/imports/<id>/discard` (`{"download_id": …, "blocklist": true}`). Errors of an app answer `504` (timeout), `503` (no connection) or `502`. A wrong API key of an app is `502` "Invalid API key", not `401`: the page treats every `401` as an expired session.
```

(5) Vor `## Upgrading to 0.9.0` (Zeile 190) einfügen:

```markdown
## Upgrading to 0.10.0

- No database change. Back up `./data` anyway, as before every upgrade.
- The new **Imports** page reads the queues of the enabled instances live: on every visit, and for the counter in the menu at most once a minute per app (`GET /api/v3/queue/status` and `GET /api/v3/system/status` while nothing waits; the queue only when a warning or error flag is set).
- Import really moves files into your library (`importMode` `auto`), and Discard removes the download and its files from the download client. Both ask first.
- Behind a reverse proxy, allow a read timeout of at least 180 seconds for `/api/imports/`: the app runs ffprobe before it answers, and an import reads the proposal again before it sends the command.
- "Queue in Sonarr" / "Queue in Radarr" needs the apps' ports to be reachable on the same host as missingarr.
- If an external script or notification links to `/imports`, you can switch off the app's own notification "On Manual Interaction Required" in Radarr and Sonarr.

```

- [ ] **Step 2: Stand prüfen**

```bash
git status --short
git log --oneline 24aee3a..HEAD
```

Erwartet: `git status` zeigt nichts (liegt diese Plandatei noch uncommittet da, entscheidet der Betreiber über ihren `docs:`-Commit). `git log` zeigt die acht Commits der Tasks, höchstens dazu den Plan-Commit.

- [ ] **Step 3: Push-Prüfung**

Nur die geänderten Zeilen außerhalb von `docs/`:

```bash
git diff -U0 24aee3a..HEAD -- . ':(exclude)docs' | grep '^+' | grep -nE '/root/|/home/|/tmp/|/mnt/|/srv/'
git diff -U0 24aee3a..HEAD -- . ':(exclude)docs' | grep '^+' | grep -nE '([0-9]{1,3}\.){3}[0-9]{1,3}' | grep -vE '127\.0\.0\.1|"4\.0\.0\.0"'
git diff -U0 24aee3a..HEAD -- . ':(exclude)docs' | grep '^+' | grep -nE '[?&]apikey=[0-9a-fA-F]{20,}|X-Api-Key:[[:space:]]*[0-9a-fA-F]{20,}'
```

Erwartet: keine Ausgabe (die erste Zeile meldet nur Host-Pfade, die zweite IPs außer `127.0.0.1` und der Versionsnummer im Test-Doppel, die dritte echte Schlüssel in URLs oder Headern). Dazu einmal von Hand die neuen Dateien auf Personennamen, Hostnamen, Indexer-Namen und Daten aus der eigenen Bibliothek durchsehen.

- [ ] **Step 4: Übergabe an den Betreiber**

Zusammenfassung: Tests, Ergebnis der Push-Prüfung, offene Punkte aus Vertrag Abschnitt 5. Ein Push des Branches geschieht nur nach seiner Freigabe.

Nicht Teil dieses Plans (eigene Schritte mit Freigabe des Betreibers, Spec „Einführung“): Codex-Durchsicht des Codes, Tag `v0.10.0` (ein Tag `v*` veröffentlicht über `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml` ein Image), GitHub-Release, Merge nach `main`, Einspielen mit Sicherung wie bei 0.9.0, und die Schritte außerhalb dieses Repos (Meldung des Auto-Imports mit Link auf `/imports`, danach die App-eigene Meldung „On Manual Interaction Required“ abschalten).
